INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM_ALL ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID , CELL_MODEL, NICK_NAME, DMS_SALES, DMS_CUSTOMER, OWNER , DEPT_ID, DEPT_NAME_KO, FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT , ITEM_TYPE, PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_OWNER, BIZ_TYPE , SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS , ITEM_NAME, VMI_YN, CUST_GROUP, CUST_GROUP_DTL, APPLICATION, ROUTE , MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY, FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, CELL_TYPE , FORECAST_INFO_ALL, FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION , CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE, MODULE_PER_CAR , SORT_ORDER_NO, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, PLANT_SITE_ID , ITEM_VALID_YN, END_CUSTOMER, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT ) SELECT F.DIVISION_ID , F.PROJECT_CODE , F.SCM_SITE_ID , F.ITEM_ID , F.ATTRIBUTE5 AS CELL_MODEL , T2.NICK_NAME , F.ATTRIBUTE1 AS DMS_SALES , F.ATTRIBUTE2 AS DMS_CUSTOMER , (SELECT LISTAGG(X.USER_NAME_EN, ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY X.USER_NAME_EN) FROM (SELECT DISTINCT ZA.USER_NAME_EN FROM (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(F.OWNER, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOK FROM DUAL CONNECT BY LEVEL <= REGEXP_COUNT(F.OWNER, ',') + 1) TK JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO ZA ON ZA.USER_ID = TK.TOK) X ) AS OWNER , TEAM.DEPT_ID , TEAM.DEPT_NAME_KO , CASE WHEN F.ITEM_ID = LOOKA.MODEL THEN TO_NUMBER(LOOKA.CELL_PACK_COUNT DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) ELSE T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT END AS FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT , T2.ITEM_TYPE , NVL(MP.PROJECT_NAME, F.PROJECT_CODE) AS PROJECT_NAME , J.USER_NAME_EN AS PROJECT_OWNER , '' AS BIZ_TYPE , S.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER , I.COUNTRY_NAME , H.CITY_NAME , '' AS PROJECT_STATUS , T2.ITEM_NAME , S.VMI_YN , F.CUST_GROUP , F.CUST_GROUP_DTL , F.APPLICATION , F.ROUTE , T2.MDM_PROJECT_NAME , TO_NUMBER(T2.MIN_CAPACITY DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS MIN_CAPACITY , CASE WHEN F.ITEM_ID = LOOKA.MODEL THEN LOOKA.BIZ_TYPE ELSE T2.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE END AS FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE , LOOK.NAME AS CELL_TYPE , T2.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC || T2.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION AS FORECAST_INFO_ALL , T2.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC , T2.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION , T4.NICK_NAME AS CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME , TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_CAR DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS CELL_PER_CAR , TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_MODULE DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS CELL_PER_MODULE , TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_CAR DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) / CASE WHEN NVL(TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_MODULE DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR), 0) = 0 THEN 1 ELSE TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_MODULE DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) END AS MODULE_PER_CAR , NULL AS SORT_ORDER_NO , TO_CHAR(T2.ENERGE) AS ENERGE , TO_NUMBER(T2.CELL_QUANTITY DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS CELL_QUANTITY , '-' AS PLANT_SITE_ID , F.VALID_YN AS ITEM_VALID_YN , S.END_CUSTOMER , NULL AS PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM F INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V T2 ON F.DIVISION_ID = T2.DIVISION_ID AND F.ITEM_ID = T2.ITEM_ID INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE S ON F.DIVISION_ID = S.DIVISION_ID AND F.SCM_SITE_ID = S.SCM_SITE_ID AND S.SITE_TYPE = 'SALES' INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_CITY H ON S.CITY = H.CITY INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_COUNTRY I ON S.COUNTRY = I.COUNTRY LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_PROJECT MP ON F.DIVISION_ID = MP.DIVISION_ID AND F.PROJECT_CODE = MP.PROJECT_CODE LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO TEAM ON TEAM.DEPT_ID = F.DEPT_ID LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO J ON J.USER_ID = CASE WHEN INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') = 0 THEN F.OWNER ELSE SUBSTR(F.OWNER, 1, INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') - 1) END LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO K ON K.DEPT_ID = F.DEPT_ID LEFT JOIN ( SELECT SEGMENT1 AS CODE, SEGMENT2 AS NAME FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL WHERE LOOKUP_TYPE = 'CELL_TYPE' ) LOOK ON LOOK.CODE = T2.CELL_TYPE LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V T4 ON T2.DIVISION_ID = T4.DIVISION_ID AND T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL = T4.ITEM_ID LEFT JOIN ( SELECT A.MODEL, A.BIZ_TYPE, A.CELL_PACK_COUNT, A.MODEL_RANK FROM ( SELECT SEGMENT2 AS MODEL , SEGMENT3 AS BIZ_TYPE , SEGMENT4 AS CELL_PACK_COUNT , RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY SE
LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DM_BIZ_TYPE_40' AND SEGMENT1 = '40' ) A WHERE MODEL_RANK = 1 ) LOOKA ON LOOKA.MODEL = F.ITEM_ID WHERE F.DIVISION_ID = :B1 

END OF STMT

INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_LAST_DATA_SETUP_PRICE_C (DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, CREATE_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY) SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID , A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE , MAX(A.CREATION_DATE) AS CREATE_DATE , MAX(A.LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE_C A INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM C ON C.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID AND C.PROJECT_CODE = A.PROJECT_CODE AND C.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID AND C.ITEM_ID = A.ITEM_ID INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_VERSION V ON V.VERSION_NO = A.VERSION_NO WHERE A.DIVISION_ID = :B3 AND A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B2 AND :B1 GROUP BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID , A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE

END OF STMT

MERGE /*+ LEADING(B) USE_HASH(A) */ INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE A USING ( SELECT NVL(DIVISION_ID, '-') AS DIVISION_ID , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '-') AS SCM_SITE_ID , NVL(ITEM_ID, '-') AS ITEM_ID , NVL(DOM_EXP, '-') AS DOM_EXP , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '-') AS FCST_TYPE , MIN(CURRENCY) AS CURRENCY FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE WHERE MEASURE = 'LOC' AND CURRENCY IS NOT NULL AND YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B2 AND :B1 GROUP BY NVL(DIVISION_ID, '-') , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '-') , NVL(ITEM_ID, '-') , NVL(DOM_EXP, '-') , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '-') HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT CURRENCY) = 1 ) B ON ( NVL(A.DIVISION_ID, '-') = B.DIVISION_ID AND NVL(A.SCM_SITE_ID, '-') = B.SCM_SITE_ID AND NVL(A.ITEM_ID, '-') = B.ITEM_ID AND NVL(A.DOM_EXP, '-') = B.DOM_EXP AND NVL(A.FCST_TYPE, '-') = B.FCST_TYPE ) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET A.CURRENCY = B.CURRENCY WHERE A.CURRENCY IS NULL AND A.MEASURE NOT LIKE 'VMI%' AND A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B3 AND :B1 

END OF STMT

INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE ( USER_GROUP, DIVISION_ID, MEASURE, DAYS, FROM_TIME, TO_TIME, LIMIT_MEASURE ) SELECT B.USER_GROUP, B.DIVISION_ID, B.MEASURE, B.DAYS, B.FROM_TIME, B.TO_TIME, LM.TOKEN AS LIMIT_MEASURE FROM (SELECT A.SEGMENT1 AS USER_GROUP, A.SEGMENT3 AS DIVISION_ID, A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE, DT.TOKEN AS DAYS , A.ATTRIBUTE2 AS FROM_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE3 AS TO_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE4 AS LIMIT_MEASURE FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(A.ATTRIBUTE1, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN FROM DUAL CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(A.ATTRIBUTE1, ','), 0) + 1) DT WHERE A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE' AND A.VALID_YN = 'Y' AND (A.SEGMENT1 = :B3 OR A.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G WHERE G.USER_ID IN (:B3 , :B4 ) AND G.USE_YN = 'Y')) AND A.SEGMENT2 = :B2 AND A.SEGMENT3 = :B1 ) B CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN FROM DUAL CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, ','), 0) + 1) LM WHERE (REPLACE(B.DAYS, ' ', '') IS NULL OR B.DAYS = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH')) AND (REPLACE(B.FROM_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL OR B.FROM_TIME <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI')) AND (REPLACE(B.TO_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL OR B.TO_TIME >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))

END OF STMT

T.PROJECT_CODE = S.PROJECT_CODE AND T.SCM_SITE_ID = S.SCM_SITE_ID AND T.ITEM_ID = S.ITEM_ID AND T.DOM_EXP = S.DOM_EXP AND T.FCST_TYPE = S.FCST_TYPE AND T.INCOTERMS = S.INCOTERMS) WHEN MATCHED THEN MERGE INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP T USING ( SELECT NVL(DIVISION_ID, '~') AS DIVISION_ID , NVL(PROJECT_CODE, '~') AS PROJECT_CODE , NVL(END_CUSTOMER, '~') AS END_CUSTOMER , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '~') AS SCM_SITE_ID , NVL(ITEM_ID, '~') AS ITEM_ID , NVL(DOM_EXP, '~') AS DOM_EXP , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '~') AS FCST_TYPE , NVL(INCOTERMS, '~') AS INCOTERMS , NVL(PLANT_SITE_ID, '~') AS PLANT_SITE_ID , NVL(MEASURE, '~') AS MEASURE , MAX(CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) KEEP (DENSE_RANK LAST ORDER BY CASE WHEN BUCKET_KEY >= :B1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END , BUCKET_KEY) AS REP_CELL_PROD FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP WHERE CELL_PROD_SITE_ID IS NOT NULL GROUP BY NVL(DIVISION_ID, '~') , NVL(PROJECT_CODE, '~') , NVL(END_CUSTOMER, '~') , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '~') , NVL(ITEM_ID, '~') , NVL(DOM_EXP, '~') , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '~') , NVL(INCOTERMS, '~') , NVL(PLANT_SITE_ID, '~') , NVL(MEASURE, '~') HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) > 1 ) S ON ( NVL(T.DIVISION_ID, '~') = S.DIVISION_ID AND NVL(T.PROJECT_CODE, '~') = S.PROJECT_CODE AND NVL(T.END_CUSTOMER, '~') = S.END_CUSTOMER AND NVL(T.SCM_SITE_ID, '~') = S.SCM_SITE_ID AND NVL(T.ITEM_ID, '~') = S.ITEM_ID AND NVL(T.DOM_EXP, '~') = S.DOM_EXP AND NVL(T.FCST_TYPE, '~') = S.FCST_TYPE AND NVL(T.INCOTERMS, '~') = S.INCOTERMS AND NVL(T.PLANT_SITE_ID, '~') = S.PLANT_SITE_ID AND NVL(T.MEASURE, '~') = S.MEASURE ) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = S.REP_CELL_PROD WHERE NVL(T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, '~') <> S.REP_CELL_PROD

END OF STMT

        FROM ( SELECT MSP.*, MSP.DEPT_ID AS TEAM_ID, MSP.DEPT_NAME_KO AS TEAM_NAME_KO /*[UICATEGORY 2026-08-07]*/, MSP.CELL_MODEL AS FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL, NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.CREATE_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), ' ') AS LAST_UPDATE_DATE, NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.SUN),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.MON),'0')
SP.FRI),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.SAT),'0') AS BUCKET FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP MSP ) F
        WHERE ((MEASURE NOT IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP') AND QTY <> 0) OR MEASURE IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP'))

        SELECT DIVISION_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, PROJECT_CODE, AS_FROM_SITE, PLANT_SITE_ID, TEAM_ID, MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, SORT_ORDER_NO, 'W_' || YYYYMMWW AS PIVOT_BUCKET

        FROM ( SELECT MSP.*, MSP.DEPT_ID AS TEAM_ID, MSP.DEPT_NAME_KO AS TEAM_NAME_KO /*[UICATEGORY 2026-08-07]*/, MSP.CELL_MODEL AS FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL, NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.CREATE_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), ' ') AS LAST_UPDATE_DATE, NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.SUN),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.MON),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.TUE),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.WED),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.THU),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.FRI),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.SAT),'0') AS BUCKET FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP MSP ) F
